        <div class="positioning-copy">
          <p>Personal image is not about following every trend or becoming someone else. It is about understanding yourself, recognising what works for you and expressing that identity with confidence.</p>
          <p>Not Normal Style brings together wardrobe, colour, proportion and personal style to create an image that feels authentic, intentional and entirely your own.</p>
        </div>
        <a class="button button-dark" href="about.html">Meet Camila Rades</a>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="section split-section homepage-philosophy" id="philosophy">
      <div>
        <p class="eyebrow">The philosophy</p>
        <h2 class="philosophy-title"><span>Style with intention.</span><span>Never by imitation.</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="philosophy-copy">
        <p>There is no single definition of beauty and no universal formula for personal style.</p>
        <p>Your image should reflect your personality, support your lifestyle and evolve with you. The purpose is not to fit into someone else’s expectations. It is to recognise what makes you distinctive and learn how to express it with clarity.</p>
        <p>Not Normal Style offers personalised guidance without imposing rules, disguising individuality or chasing temporary trends.</p>
        <p class="philosophy-emphasis">The goal is not to change who you are. It is to make who you are more visible.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="section method-section homepage-method" id="process">

      <h2 class="method-title"><span>From identity</span> <span>to expression.</span></h2>
      <p class="method-intro">A personal image becomes meaningful when it connects who you are, how you live and how you want to be seen.</p>
      <div class="process-grid">
        <article><span>01</span><h3>Discover</h3><p>Understand your identity, lifestyle, preferences, natural features and goals.</p></article>
        <article><span>02</span><h3>Define</h3><p>Create a clear direction for colour, silhouette, wardrobe and personal style.</p></article>
        <article><span>03</span><h3>Express</h3><p>Translate that direction into outfits, shopping choices and confident everyday decisions.</p></article>
      </div>

    <section class="about-hero about-meet" id="meet-camila">
      <div>
        <p class="eyebrow">Meet Camila Rades</p>
        <p class="about-kicker">Founder of Not Normal Style · Personal Image Consultant</p>
        <h1 class="about-identity-title"><span>Style begins</span><span>with identity.</span></h1>
        <blockquote class="about-quote"><p>“I believe that when you feel good in what you wear, you feel more confident in who you are.”</p><cite>Camila Rades</cite></blockquote>
        
      </div>
      <div class="about-portrait"><img src="images/hero-camila.jpg" alt="Camila Rades, personal image consultant"></div>
      
    </section>

    <section class="about-chapter" id="where-it-began">
      <div class="chapter-number">01</div>
      <div class="chapter-heading">
        <p class="eyebrow">Where it all began</p>
        <h2 class="about-story-title"><span>The story behind</span><span>Not Normal Style.</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="chapter-copy">
        <p>My interest in personal image began long before I knew it could become my profession.</p>
        <p>As a child, I watched a film in which a character put on a jacket and appeared transformed. What stayed with me was not simply the change in appearance, but the confidence that came with it.</p>
        <p>That moment introduced an idea that has remained with me ever since: clothing can help people recognise their beauty, express their personality and carry themselves differently.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="about-chapter chapter-dark" id="experience">
      <div class="chapter-number">02</div>
      <div class="chapter-heading">
        <p class="eyebrow">Experience & expertise</p>
        <h2 class="about-experience-title"><span>Expertise shaped</span><span>by more than two</span><span>decades in fashion.</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="chapter-copy">
        <p>A degree in Fashion Design, a specialisation in Image Consulting and more than 20 years in the fashion industry have shaped a practice grounded in creativity, technical knowledge and a precise understanding of style, proportion and colour.</p>
        <p>An MBA in Luxury Management strengthened my understanding of how people, brands and services create value. It influences how I structure each consultation, communicate recommendations and design an experience that feels clear, considered and genuinely useful.</p>
        <p>Together, these foundations support a thoughtful and highly personal approach, helping each client understand what suits them and express who they are with clarity and confidence.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="about-chapter" id="why-not-normal">
      <div class="chapter-number">03</div>
      <div class="chapter-heading">
        <p class="eyebrow">Why Not Normal Style?</p>
        <h2 class="about-fit-title"><span>Not made</span><span>to fit in.</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="chapter-copy">
        <p>Not Normal Style grew from a desire to make personal image consulting feel more approachable, relevant and genuinely personal.</p>
        <p>The Instagram space became an early way to share practical knowledge, offer visual inspiration and open conversations about style beyond trends and expectations. What began as a place to communicate those ideas gradually developed into a broader consulting practice.</p>
        <p>The name reflects the belief at the heart of the brand: personal style should not require someone to follow a formula or disappear into somebody else’s idea of what looks right.</p>
        <p>“Not normal” is not about standing apart for the sake of it. It is about recognising what makes each person individual and giving that identity space to be seen. Because being stylish does not mean looking like everyone else. It means looking and feeling like yourself.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="about-chapter chapter-dark" id="philosophy">
      <div class="chapter-number">04</div>
      <div class="chapter-heading">
        <p class="eyebrow">The Not Normal philosophy</p>
        <h2 class="about-philosophy-title"><span>Your style.</span><span>Your rules.</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="chapter-copy">
        <p>My role is not to impose an image or replace one identity with another. It is to help each client see themselves more clearly and make choices that feel natural, intentional and authentic.</p>
        <p>Colour, silhouette and wardrobe are part of that process, but the experience goes further. It is about developing a more confident relationship with your image and understanding how to express who you are in daily life.</p>
        <p>There is no single definition of beauty and no universal formula for personal style. The goal is not to change who you are. It is to make who you are more visible.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="about-closing about-closing-centred">
      <p class="eyebrow">Not Normal Style · Personal Image Consulting</p>
      <h2 class="about-closing-title"><span class="closing-line"><span class="closing-phrase">Not made</span><span class="closing-phrase">to fit in.</span></span><span class="closing-line"><span class="closing-phrase">Made to</span><span class="closing-phrase">stand out.</span></span></h2>
      <p class="about-closing-copy">Discover a personal image that feels considered, confident and entirely your own.</p>
      <div class="closing-actions">

/*
  ==================================================
  EDITORIAL HERO WITH PHOTOGRAPH
  ==================================================

  On larger screens, the hero uses two columns:
  - the text occupies the left column;
  - Camila's photograph occupies the right column.
*/

.hero {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1.1fr 0.9fr;
  gap: 64px;
  align-items: center;
}

/*
  Keeps the text comfortably readable.
*/
.hero-content {
  max-width: 720px;
}

/*
  Creates a tall editorial frame for the photograph.
  "overflow: hidden" hides any part extending beyond it.
*/
.hero-image {
  height: min(72vh, 780px);
  min-height: 520px;
  overflow: hidden;
  background-color: #e4e1d8;
}

/*
  Makes the image fill its frame without stretching.

  "object-fit: cover" may crop part of the photograph
  while preserving its original proportions.
*/
.hero-image img {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  object-fit: cover;
  object-position: center;
}

/*
  MOBILE HERO

  Below 760 pixels, the layout changes from two columns
  to one column. The text appears above the photograph.
*/
@media (max-width: 760px) {
  .hero {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 48px;
  }

  .hero-image {
    height: 70vh;
    min-height: 440px;
  }
}

    <section class="about-chapter" id="where-it-began">
      <div class="chapter-number">01</div>
      <div class="chapter-heading">
        <p class="eyebrow">Where it all began</p>
        <h2 class="about-story-title"><span>The story behind</span><span>Not Normal Style.</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="chapter-copy">
        <p>My interest in personal image began long before I knew it could become my profession.</p>
        <p>As a child, I watched a film in which a character put on a jacket and appeared transformed. What stayed with me was not simply the change in appearance, but the confidence that came with it.</p>
        <p>That moment introduced an idea that has remained with me ever since: clothing can help people recognise their beauty, express their personality and carry themselves differently.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="about-chapter chapter-dark" id="experience">
      <div class="chapter-number">02</div>
      <div class="chapter-heading">
        <p class="eyebrow">Experience & expertise</p>
        <h2 class="about-experience-title"><span>Expertise shaped</span><span>by more than two</span><span>decades in fashion.</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="chapter-copy">
        <p>A degree in Fashion Design, a specialisation in Image Consulting and more than 20 years in the fashion industry have shaped a practice grounded in creativity, technical knowledge and a precise understanding of style, proportion and colour.</p>
        <p>An MBA in Luxury Management strengthened my understanding of how people, brands and services create value. It influences how I structure each consultation, communicate recommendations and design an experience that feels clear, considered and genuinely useful.</p>
        <p>Together, these foundations support a thoughtful and highly personal approach, helping each client understand what suits them and express who they are with clarity and confidence.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="about-chapter" id="why-not-normal">
      <div class="chapter-number">03</div>
      <div class="chapter-heading">
        <p class="eyebrow">Why Not Normal Style?</p>
        <h2 class="about-fit-title"><span>Not made</span><span>to fit in.</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="chapter-copy">
        <p>Not Normal Style grew from a desire to make personal image consulting feel more approachable, relevant and genuinely personal.</p>
        <p>The Instagram space became an early way to share practical knowledge, offer visual inspiration and open conversations about style beyond trends and expectations. What began as a place to communicate those ideas gradually developed into a broader consulting practice.</p>
        <p>The name reflects the belief at the heart of the brand: personal style should not require someone to follow a formula or disappear into somebody else’s idea of what looks right.</p>
        <p>“Not normal” is not about standing apart for the sake of it. It is about recognising what makes each person individual and giving that identity space to be seen. Because being stylish does not mean looking like everyone else. It means looking and feeling like yourself.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="about-chapter chapter-dark" id="philosophy">
      <div class="chapter-number">04</div>
      <div class="chapter-heading">
        <p class="eyebrow">The Not Normal philosophy</p>
        <h2 class="about-philosophy-title"><span>Your style.</span><span>Your rules.</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="chapter-copy">
        <p>My role is not to impose an image or replace one identity with another. It is to help each client see themselves more clearly and make choices that feel natural, intentional and authentic.</p>
        <p>Colour, silhouette and wardrobe are part of that process, but the experience goes further. It is about developing a more confident relationship with your image and understanding how to express who you are in daily life.</p>
        <p>There is no single definition of beauty and no universal formula for personal style. The goal is not to change who you are. It is to make who you are more visible.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

===== FILE: ./backup-before-performance-package-20261109-230855/js/main.js =====
const yearElement = document.querySelector("#current-year");
const menuButton = document.querySelector(".menu-button");
const navigation = document.querySelector("#main-navigation");

if (yearElement) {
  yearElement.textContent = new Date().getFullYear();
}

if (menuButton && navigation) {
  menuButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const isOpen = navigation.classList.toggle("is-open");
    menuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
  });

  navigation.querySelectorAll("a").forEach((link) => {
    link.addEventListener("click", () => {
      navigation.classList.remove("is-open");
      menuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
    });
  });
}


/* Keep only one detailed service open at a time. */
const serviceItems = document.querySelectorAll(".service-item");
serviceItems.forEach((currentItem) => {
  currentItem.addEventListener("toggle", () => {
    if (!currentItem.open) return;
    serviceItems.forEach((otherItem) => {
      if (otherItem !== currentItem) otherItem.open = false;
    });
  });
});


/* Services category submenu. */
const submenuButton = document.querySelector(".submenu-button");
const servicesSubmenu = document.querySelector("#services-submenu");

if (submenuButton && servicesSubmenu) {
  const closeServicesSubmenu = () => {
    servicesSubmenu.classList.remove("is-open");
    submenuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  };

  submenuButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const isOpen = servicesSubmenu.classList.toggle("is-open");
    submenuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
  });

  servicesSubmenu.querySelectorAll("a").forEach((link) => {
    link.addEventListener("click", closeServicesSubmenu);
  });

  document.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
    if (event.key === "Escape") {
      closeServicesSubmenu();
      submenuButton.focus();
    }
  });
}


/* Consultation form validation. */
const consultationForm = document.querySelector("#consultation-form");
if (consultationForm) {
  const status = document.querySelector("#form-status");

  const setError = (field, message) => {
    const error = document.querySelector(`#${field.id}-error`);
    field.setAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
    if (error) error.textContent = message;
  };

  const clearErrors = () => {
    consultationForm.querySelectorAll("[aria-invalid='true']").forEach((field) => field.removeAttribute("aria-invalid"));
    consultationForm.querySelectorAll(".field-error").forEach((error) => error.textContent = "");
    status.textContent = "";
  };

  consultationForm.addEventListener("submit", (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    clearErrors();
    let firstInvalid = null;

    const name = consultationForm.elements["full-name"];
    const email = consultationForm.elements.email;
    const confirmEmail = consultationForm.elements["confirm-email"];
    const language = consultationForm.elements.language;
    const message = consultationForm.elements.message;
    const privacy = consultationForm.elements["privacy-acknowledgement"];
    const interest = consultationForm.querySelector("input[name='interest']:checked");

    if (!name.value.trim()) { setError(name, "Please enter your name."); firstInvalid ||= name; }
    if (!email.value.trim() || !email.validity.valid) { setError(email, "Please enter a valid email address, for example name@example.com."); firstInvalid ||= email; }
    if (!confirmEmail.value.trim() || confirmEmail.value.trim().toLowerCase() !== email.value.trim().toLowerCase()) { setError(confirmEmail, "The email addresses must match."); firstInvalid ||= confirmEmail; }
    if (!language.value) { setError(language, "Please select your preferred language."); firstInvalid ||= language; }
    if (!interest) { document.querySelector("#interest-error").textContent = "Please choose what brings you here."; firstInvalid ||= consultationForm.querySelector("input[name='interest']"); }
    if (message.value.trim().length < 20) { setError(message, "Please add at least 20 characters so Camila Rades can understand your enquiry."); firstInvalid ||= message; }
    if (!privacy.checked) { setError(privacy, "Please confirm that you have read the Privacy Policy."); firstInvalid ||= privacy; }

    if (firstInvalid) {
      status.textContent = "Please review the highlighted fields.";
      firstInvalid.focus();
      return;
    }

    status.textContent = "The form is complete. Delivery is not connected in this local preview, so no information has been sent.";
  });
}

    <article class="legal-page">
      <header class="legal-hero">
        <p class="eyebrow">GDPR information</p>
        <h1>Privacy Policy.</h1>
        <p>Last updated: <span class="legal-placeholder">[INSERT DATE]</span></p>
      </header>

      <div class="legal-introduction">
        <p>This Privacy Policy explains how personal data is handled by Not Normal Style in accordance with the General Data Protection Regulation (EU) 2016/679 (GDPR) and applicable Polish data-protection law.</p>
        <p class="legal-draft-note"><strong>Draft completion notice:</strong> fields in square brackets must be completed before the consultation form is connected and published.</p>
      </div>

      <section><h2>1. Data controller</h2><p>The controller of personal data is <span class="legal-placeholder">[FULL LEGAL NAME OR REGISTERED BUSINESS NAME]</span>, trading as Not Normal Style.</p><ul><li>Business or correspondence address: <span class="legal-placeholder">[ADDRESS]</span></li><li>Privacy contact email: <span class="legal-placeholder">[EMAIL]</span></li><li>Registration or tax details, where applicable: <span class="legal-placeholder">[DETAILS]</span></li></ul></section>

      <section><h2>2. Personal data we collect</h2><p>When you submit a consultation enquiry, we may collect your name, email address, country or city, preferred language, consultation interests, preferred consultation format, timing, referral source and information you choose to include in your message. We may also process technical information necessary to operate and secure the website and form.</p><p>Please do not submit health information, identity documents, payment details or other sensitive personal information through the initial consultation form.</p></section>

      <section><h2>3. Purposes of processing</h2><p>We process personal data to review and understand enquiries, respond to prospective clients, recommend a suitable service or next step, arrange introductory conversations, maintain necessary business correspondence, and protect the website and enquiry process from misuse or security threats.</p></section>

      <section><h2>4. Legal bases under the GDPR</h2><p>Depending on the nature and stage of your enquiry, processing may be necessary to take steps at your request before entering into a contract under Article 6(1)(b) GDPR, or necessary for our legitimate interests in responding to enquiries and managing business correspondence under Article 6(1)(f) GDPR.</p><p>Where we request consent for a separate optional purpose, such as marketing communication, processing will be based on Article 6(1)(a) GDPR. Consent may be withdrawn at any time without affecting processing carried out lawfully before withdrawal.</p></section>

      <section><h2>5. Requirement to provide data</h2><p>You are not legally required to submit a consultation enquiry. However, information marked as required is necessary for us to understand and respond to your request. Without this information, we may be unable to respond or recommend a suitable next step.</p></section>

      <section><h2>6. Recipients and service providers</h2><p>Personal data may be accessed by Not Normal Style and by providers that support website hosting, form delivery, email, scheduling, security or other necessary business operations.</p><ul><li>Website hosting: GitHub Pages</li><li>Form processing: <span class="legal-placeholder">[FORM PROVIDER]</span></li><li>Email provider: <span class="legal-placeholder">[EMAIL PROVIDER]</span></li><li>Scheduling provider, if used: <span class="legal-placeholder">[SCHEDULING PROVIDER OR NOT USED]</span></li><li>Analytics provider, if used: <span class="legal-placeholder">[ANALYTICS PROVIDER OR NOT USED]</span></li></ul></section>

      <section><h2>7. Transfers outside the EEA</h2><p>Some providers may process personal data outside Poland or the European Economic Area. Where required, transfers will rely on an applicable adequacy decision, approved contractual safeguards or another lawful transfer mechanism under the GDPR. This section will be finalised after the providers listed above are selected.</p></section>

      <section><h2>8. Retention</h2><p>Consultation enquiries that do not result in a service will be deleted or anonymised after <span class="legal-placeholder">[INSERT RETENTION PERIOD, FOR EXAMPLE 12 MONTHS]</span>, unless a longer period is necessary to establish, exercise or defend legal claims or comply with legal obligations.</p><p>Business, contractual, tax and payment records may be retained for periods required by applicable law.</p></section>

      <section><h2>9. Your rights</h2><p>Subject to the conditions of the GDPR, you may have the right to request access to your personal data, correction of inaccurate data, deletion, restriction of processing, data portability, or to object to certain processing. Where processing is based on consent, you may withdraw that consent at any time.</p><p>To exercise a right, contact <span class="legal-placeholder">[PRIVACY EMAIL]</span>.</p><p>You also have the right to lodge a complaint with a competent supervisory authority. In Poland, the supervisory authority is the President of the Personal Data Protection Office (UODO), ul. Stanisława Moniuszki 1A, 00-014 Warsaw, Poland.</p></section>

      <section><h2>10. Automated decision-making</h2><p>Not Normal Style does not intend to make decisions that produce legal or similarly significant effects based solely on automated processing. Any future service-matching questionnaire will provide guidance only and will not determine eligibility for a service without human involvement.</p></section>

      <section><h2>11. Security</h2><p>Reasonable technical and organisational measures will be used to protect personal data against unauthorised access, loss, misuse, alteration or disclosure. No electronic transmission or storage method is entirely risk-free, so visitors should avoid including unnecessary sensitive information in an initial enquiry.</p></section>

      <section><h2>12. Marketing</h2><p>Submitting a consultation enquiry does not subscribe you to marketing communications. If newsletters or promotional communication are introduced, separate permission will be requested where required and an unsubscribe method will be provided.</p></section>

      <section><h2>13. Cookies and analytics</h2><p>The policy will be updated to reflect the tools actually used by the website. Non-essential analytics, advertising, embedded scheduling, social-media content or other tracking technologies will not be enabled without appropriate disclosure and, where required, a valid consent mechanism.</p></section>

      <section><h2>14. Policy changes</h2><p>This Privacy Policy may be updated when services, providers or legal obligations change. The current version will be identified by the “Last updated” date at the top of this page.</p></section>

      <section><h2>15. Contact</h2><p><span class="legal-placeholder">[FULL LEGAL OR BUSINESS NAME]</span><br><span class="legal-placeholder">[PRIVACY EMAIL]</span><br><span class="legal-placeholder">[BUSINESS OR CORRESPONDENCE ADDRESS]</span></p></section>

===== FILE: ./js/main.js =====
const yearElement = document.querySelector("#current-year");
const menuButton = document.querySelector(".menu-button");
const navigation = document.querySelector("#main-navigation");

if (yearElement) {
  yearElement.textContent = new Date().getFullYear();
}

if (menuButton && navigation) {
  menuButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const isOpen = navigation.classList.toggle("is-open");
    menuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
  });

  navigation.querySelectorAll("a").forEach((link) => {
    link.addEventListener("click", () => {
      navigation.classList.remove("is-open");
      menuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
    });
  });
}


/* Keep only one detailed service open at a time. */
const serviceItems = document.querySelectorAll(".service-item");
serviceItems.forEach((currentItem) => {
  currentItem.addEventListener("toggle", () => {
    if (!currentItem.open) return;
    serviceItems.forEach((otherItem) => {
      if (otherItem !== currentItem) otherItem.open = false;
    });
  });
});


/* Services category submenu. */
const submenuButton = document.querySelector(".submenu-button");
const servicesSubmenu = document.querySelector("#services-submenu");

if (submenuButton && servicesSubmenu) {
  const closeServicesSubmenu = () => {
    servicesSubmenu.classList.remove("is-open");
    submenuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  };

  submenuButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const isOpen = servicesSubmenu.classList.toggle("is-open");
    submenuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
  });

  servicesSubmenu.querySelectorAll("a").forEach((link) => {
    link.addEventListener("click", closeServicesSubmenu);
  });

  document.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
    if (event.key === "Escape") {
      closeServicesSubmenu();
      submenuButton.focus();
    }
  });
}


/* Consultation form validation. */
const consultationForm = document.querySelector("#consultation-form");
if (consultationForm) {
  const status = document.querySelector("#form-status");

  const setError = (field, message) => {
    const error = document.querySelector(`#${field.id}-error`);
    field.setAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
    if (error) error.textContent = message;
  };

  const clearErrors = () => {
    consultationForm.querySelectorAll("[aria-invalid='true']").forEach((field) => field.removeAttribute("aria-invalid"));
    consultationForm.querySelectorAll(".field-error").forEach((error) => error.textContent = "");
    status.textContent = "";
  };

  consultationForm.addEventListener("submit", (event) => {
    clearErrors();
    let firstInvalid = null;

    const name = consultationForm.elements["full-name"];
    const email = consultationForm.elements.email;
    const confirmEmail = consultationForm.elements["confirm-email"];
    const language = consultationForm.elements.language;
    const message = consultationForm.elements.message;
    const privacy = consultationForm.elements["privacy-acknowledgement"];
    const interests = consultationForm.querySelectorAll("input[name='interest']:checked");

    if (!name.value.trim()) { setError(name, "Please enter your name."); firstInvalid ||= name; }
    if (!email.value.trim() || !email.validity.valid) { setError(email, "Please enter a valid email address, for example name@example.com."); firstInvalid ||= email; }
    if (!confirmEmail.value.trim() || confirmEmail.value.trim().toLowerCase() !== email.value.trim().toLowerCase()) { setError(confirmEmail, "The email addresses must match."); firstInvalid ||= confirmEmail; }
    if (!language.value) { setError(language, "Please select your preferred language."); firstInvalid ||= language; }
    if (!interests.length) { document.querySelector("#interest-error").textContent = "Please select at least one option."; firstInvalid ||= consultationForm.querySelector("input[name='interest']"); }
    
    if (!privacy.checked) { setError(privacy, "Please confirm that you have read the Privacy Policy."); firstInvalid ||= privacy; }

if (firstInvalid) {
  event.preventDefault();

  status.textContent = "Please review the highlighted fields.";
  firstInvalid.focus();
  return;
}

event.preventDefault();

status.textContent = "Sending your enquiry...";

const modal = document.querySelector("#consultation-success-modal");
const closeButton = document.querySelector("#close-success-modal");

fetch("https://formspree.io/f/mppdwrvz", {
  method: "POST",
  body: new FormData(consultationForm),
  headers: {
    Accept: "application/json"
  }
})
.then((response) => {
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error("Submission failed.");
  }

  consultationForm.reset();

  if (modal) {
    modal.style.display = "flex";

    const modalTitle =
      document.querySelector("#success-modal-title");

    if (modalTitle) {
      modalTitle.focus();
    }
  }

  status.textContent = "";
})
.catch(() => {
  status.textContent =
    "Something went wrong while sending your enquiry. Please try again or contact us by email.";
});

const previousFocus = document.activeElement;

const closeModal = () => {
  if (modal) {
    modal.style.display = "none";
  }

  if (previousFocus) {
    previousFocus.focus();
  }
};

if (closeButton) {
  closeButton.addEventListener("click", closeModal);
}

if (modal) {
  modal.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
    if (event.target === modal) {
      closeModal();
    }
  });
}

document.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
  if (
    event.key === "Escape" &&
    modal &&
    modal.style.display === "flex"
  ) {
    closeModal();
  }
});

return;
  });
}

        <h1>Privacy Policy.</h1>
        <p>Last updated: <span class="legal-placeholder">[INSERT DATE]</span></p>
      </header>

      <div class="legal-introduction">
        <p>This Privacy Policy explains how personal data is handled by Not Normal Style in accordance with the General Data Protection Regulation (EU) 2016/679 (GDPR) and applicable Polish data-protection law.</p>
        <p class="legal-draft-note"><strong>Draft completion notice:</strong> fields in square brackets must be completed before the consultation form is connected and published.</p>
      </div>

      <section><h2>1. Data controller</h2><p>The controller of personal data is <span class="legal-placeholder">[FULL LEGAL NAME OR REGISTERED BUSINESS NAME]</span>, trading as Not Normal Style.</p><ul><li>Business or correspondence address: <span class="legal-placeholder">[ADDRESS]</span></li><li>Privacy contact email: <span class="legal-placeholder">[EMAIL]</span></li><li>Registration or tax details, where applicable: <span class="legal-placeholder">[DETAILS]</span></li></ul></section>

      <section><h2>2. Personal data we collect</h2><p>When you submit a consultation enquiry, we may collect your name, email address, country or city, preferred language, consultation interests, preferred consultation format, timing, referral source and information you choose to include in your message. We may also process technical information necessary to operate and secure the website and form.</p><p>Please do not submit health information, identity documents, payment details or other sensitive personal information through the initial consultation form.</p></section>

      <section><h2>3. Purposes of processing</h2><p>We process personal data to review and understand enquiries, respond to prospective clients, recommend a suitable service or next step, arrange introductory conversations, maintain necessary business correspondence, and protect the website and enquiry process from misuse or security threats.</p></section>

      <section><h2>4. Legal bases under the GDPR</h2><p>Depending on the nature and stage of your enquiry, processing may be necessary to take steps at your request before entering into a contract under Article 6(1)(b) GDPR, or necessary for our legitimate interests in responding to enquiries and managing business correspondence under Article 6(1)(f) GDPR.</p><p>Where we request consent for a separate optional purpose, such as marketing communication, processing will be based on Article 6(1)(a) GDPR. Consent may be withdrawn at any time without affecting processing carried out lawfully before withdrawal.</p></section>

      <section><h2>5. Requirement to provide data</h2><p>You are not legally required to submit a consultation enquiry. However, information marked as required is necessary for us to understand and respond to your request. Without this information, we may be unable to respond or recommend a suitable next step.</p></section>

      <section><h2>6. Recipients and service providers</h2><p>Personal data may be accessed by Not Normal Style and by providers that support website hosting, form delivery, email, scheduling, security or other necessary business operations.</p><ul><li>Website hosting: GitHub Pages</li><li>Form processing: <span class="legal-placeholder">[FORM PROVIDER]</span></li><li>Email provider: <span class="legal-placeholder">[EMAIL PROVIDER]</span></li><li>Scheduling provider, if used: <span class="legal-placeholder">[SCHEDULING PROVIDER OR NOT USED]</span></li><li>Analytics provider, if used: <span class="legal-placeholder">[ANALYTICS PROVIDER OR NOT USED]</span></li></ul></section>

      <section><h2>7. Transfers outside the EEA</h2><p>Some providers may process personal data outside Poland or the European Economic Area. Where required, transfers will rely on an applicable adequacy decision, approved contractual safeguards or another lawful transfer mechanism under the GDPR. This section will be finalised after the providers listed above are selected.</p></section>

      <section><h2>8. Retention</h2><p>Consultation enquiries that do not result in a service will be deleted or anonymised after <span class="legal-placeholder">[INSERT RETENTION PERIOD, FOR EXAMPLE 12 MONTHS]</span>, unless a longer period is necessary to establish, exercise or defend legal claims or comply with legal obligations.</p><p>Business, contractual, tax and payment records may be retained for periods required by applicable law.</p></section>

      <section><h2>9. Your rights</h2><p>Subject to the conditions of the GDPR, you may have the right to request access to your personal data, correction of inaccurate data, deletion, restriction of processing, data portability, or to object to certain processing. Where processing is based on consent, you may withdraw that consent at any time.</p><p>To exercise a right, contact <span class="legal-placeholder">[PRIVACY EMAIL]</span>.</p><p>You also have the right to lodge a complaint with a competent supervisory authority. In Poland, the supervisory authority is the President of the Personal Data Protection Office (UODO), ul. Stanisława Moniuszki 1A, 00-014 Warsaw, Poland.</p></section>

      <section><h2>10. Automated decision-making</h2><p>Not Normal Style does not intend to make decisions that produce legal or similarly significant effects based solely on automated processing. Any future service-matching questionnaire will provide guidance only and will not determine eligibility for a service without human involvement.</p></section>

      <section><h2>11. Security</h2><p>Reasonable technical and organisational measures will be used to protect personal data against unauthorised access, loss, misuse, alteration or disclosure. No electronic transmission or storage method is entirely risk-free, so visitors should avoid including unnecessary sensitive information in an initial enquiry.</p></section>

      <section><h2>12. Marketing</h2><p>Submitting a consultation enquiry does not subscribe you to marketing communications. If newsletters or promotional communication are introduced, separate permission will be requested where required and an unsubscribe method will be provided.</p></section>

      <section><h2>13. Cookies and analytics</h2><p>The policy will be updated to reflect the tools actually used by the website. Non-essential analytics, advertising, embedded scheduling, social-media content or other tracking technologies will not be enabled without appropriate disclosure and, where required, a valid consent mechanism.</p></section>

      <section><h2>14. Policy changes</h2><p>This Privacy Policy may be updated when services, providers or legal obligations change. The current version will be identified by the “Last updated” date at the top of this page.</p></section>

        <p class="eyebrow">GDPR information</p>
        <h1>Privacy Policy.</h1>
        <p>Last updated: <span class="legal-placeholder">[INSERT DATE]</span></p>
      </header>

      <div class="legal-introduction">
        <p>This Privacy Policy explains how personal data is handled by Not Normal Style in accordance with the General Data Protection Regulation (EU) 2016/679 (GDPR) and applicable Polish data-protection law.</p>
        <p class="legal-draft-note"><strong>Draft completion notice:</strong> fields in square brackets must be completed before the consultation form is connected and published.</p>
      </div>

      <section><h2>1. Data controller</h2><p>The controller of personal data is <span class="legal-placeholder">[FULL LEGAL NAME OR REGISTERED BUSINESS NAME]</span>, trading as Not Normal Style.</p><ul><li>Business or correspondence address: <span class="legal-placeholder">[ADDRESS]</span></li><li>Privacy contact email: <span class="legal-placeholder">[EMAIL]</span></li><li>Registration or tax details, where applicable: <span class="legal-placeholder">[DETAILS]</span></li></ul></section>

      <section><h2>2. Personal data we collect</h2><p>When you submit a consultation enquiry, we may collect your name, email address, country or city, preferred language, consultation interests, preferred consultation format, timing, referral source and information you choose to include in your message. We may also process technical information necessary to operate and secure the website and form.</p><p>Please do not submit health information, identity documents, payment details or other sensitive personal information through the initial consultation form.</p></section>

      <section><h2>3. Purposes of processing</h2><p>We process personal data to review and understand enquiries, respond to prospective clients, recommend a suitable service or next step, arrange introductory conversations, maintain necessary business correspondence, and protect the website and enquiry process from misuse or security threats.</p></section>

      <section><h2>4. Legal bases under the GDPR</h2><p>Depending on the nature and stage of your enquiry, processing may be necessary to take steps at your request before entering into a contract under Article 6(1)(b) GDPR, or necessary for our legitimate interests in responding to enquiries and managing business correspondence under Article 6(1)(f) GDPR.</p><p>Where we request consent for a separate optional purpose, such as marketing communication, processing will be based on Article 6(1)(a) GDPR. Consent may be withdrawn at any time without affecting processing carried out lawfully before withdrawal.</p></section>

      <section><h2>5. Requirement to provide data</h2><p>You are not legally required to submit a consultation enquiry. However, information marked as required is necessary for us to understand and respond to your request. Without this information, we may be unable to respond or recommend a suitable next step.</p></section>

      <section><h2>6. Recipients and service providers</h2><p>Personal data may be accessed by Not Normal Style and by providers that support website hosting, form delivery, email, scheduling, security or other necessary business operations.</p><ul><li>Website hosting: GitHub Pages</li><li>Form processing: <span class="legal-placeholder">[FORM PROVIDER]</span></li><li>Email provider: <span class="legal-placeholder">[EMAIL PROVIDER]</span></li><li>Scheduling provider, if used: <span class="legal-placeholder">[SCHEDULING PROVIDER OR NOT USED]</span></li><li>Analytics provider, if used: <span class="legal-placeholder">[ANALYTICS PROVIDER OR NOT USED]</span></li></ul></section>

      <section><h2>7. Transfers outside the EEA</h2><p>Some providers may process personal data outside Poland or the European Economic Area. Where required, transfers will rely on an applicable adequacy decision, approved contractual safeguards or another lawful transfer mechanism under the GDPR. This section will be finalised after the providers listed above are selected.</p></section>

      <section><h2>8. Retention</h2><p>Consultation enquiries that do not result in a service will be deleted or anonymised after <span class="legal-placeholder">[INSERT RETENTION PERIOD, FOR EXAMPLE 12 MONTHS]</span>, unless a longer period is necessary to establish, exercise or defend legal claims or comply with legal obligations.</p><p>Business, contractual, tax and payment records may be retained for periods required by applicable law.</p></section>

      <section><h2>9. Your rights</h2><p>Subject to the conditions of the GDPR, you may have the right to request access to your personal data, correction of inaccurate data, deletion, restriction of processing, data portability, or to object to certain processing. Where processing is based on consent, you may withdraw that consent at any time.</p><p>To exercise a right, contact <span class="legal-placeholder">[PRIVACY EMAIL]</span>.</p><p>You also have the right to lodge a complaint with a competent supervisory authority. In Poland, the supervisory authority is the President of the Personal Data Protection Office (UODO), ul. Stanisława Moniuszki 1A, 00-014 Warsaw, Poland.</p></section>

      <section><h2>10. Automated decision-making</h2><p>Not Normal Style does not intend to make decisions that produce legal or similarly significant effects based solely on automated processing. Any future service-matching questionnaire will provide guidance only and will not determine eligibility for a service without human involvement.</p></section>

      <section><h2>11. Security</h2><p>Reasonable technical and organisational measures will be used to protect personal data against unauthorised access, loss, misuse, alteration or disclosure. No electronic transmission or storage method is entirely risk-free, so visitors should avoid including unnecessary sensitive information in an initial enquiry.</p></section>

      <section><h2>12. Marketing</h2><p>Submitting a consultation enquiry does not subscribe you to marketing communications. If newsletters or promotional communication are introduced, separate permission will be requested where required and an unsubscribe method will be provided.</p></section>

      <section><h2>13. Cookies and analytics</h2><p>The policy will be updated to reflect the tools actually used by the website. Non-essential analytics, advertising, embedded scheduling, social-media content or other tracking technologies will not be enabled without appropriate disclosure and, where required, a valid consent mechanism.</p></section>

      <section><h2>14. Policy changes</h2><p>This Privacy Policy may be updated when services, providers or legal obligations change. The current version will be identified by the “Last updated” date at the top of this page.</p></section>

      <section><h2>15. Contact</h2><p><span class="legal-placeholder">[FULL LEGAL OR BUSINESS NAME]</span><br><span class="legal-placeholder">[PRIVACY EMAIL]</span><br><span class="legal-placeholder">[BUSINESS OR CORRESPONDENCE ADDRESS]</span></p></section>

      </div>
      <div class="philosophy-copy">
        <p>There is no single definition of beauty and no universal formula for personal style.</p>
        <p>Your image should reflect your personality, support your lifestyle and evolve with you. The purpose is not to fit into someone else’s expectations. It is to recognise what makes you distinctive and learn how to express it with clarity.</p>
        <p>Not Normal Style offers personalised guidance without imposing rules, disguising individuality or chasing temporary trends.</p>

    <section class="about-hero about-meet" id="meet-camila">
      <div>
        <p class="eyebrow">Meet Camila Rades</p>
        <p class="about-kicker">Founder of Not Normal Style · Personal Image Consultant</p>
        <h1 class="about-identity-title"><span>Style begins</span><span>with identity.</span></h1>
        <blockquote class="about-quote"><p>“I believe that when you feel good in what you wear, you feel more confident in who you are.”</p><cite>Camila Rades</cite></blockquote>
        
      </div>
      <div class="about-portrait"><img src="images/hero-camila.jpg" alt="Camila Rades, personal image consultant" width="648" height="944"></div>
      
    </section>

    <section class="about-chapter" id="where-it-began">
      <div class="chapter-number">01</div>
      <div class="chapter-heading">
        <p class="eyebrow">Where it all began</p>
        <h2 class="about-story-title"><span>The story behind</span><span>Not Normal Style.</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="chapter-copy">
        <p>My interest in personal image began long before I knew it could become my profession.</p>
        <p>As a child, I watched a film in which a character put on a jacket and appeared transformed. What stayed with me was not simply the change in appearance, but the confidence that came with it.</p>
        <p>That moment introduced an idea that has remained with me ever since: clothing can help people recognise their beauty, express their personality and carry themselves differently.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="about-chapter chapter-dark" id="experience">
      <div class="chapter-number">02</div>
      <div class="chapter-heading">
        <p class="eyebrow">Experience & expertise</p>
        <h2 class="about-experience-title"><span>Expertise shaped</span><span>by more than two</span><span>decades in fashion.</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="chapter-copy">
        <p>A degree in Fashion Design, a specialisation in Image Consulting and more than 20 years in the fashion industry have shaped a practice grounded in creativity, technical knowledge and a precise understanding of style, proportion and colour.</p>
        <p>An MBA in Luxury Management strengthened my understanding of how people, brands and services create value. It influences how I structure each consultation, communicate recommendations and design an experience that feels clear, considered and genuinely useful.</p>
        <p>Together, these foundations support a thoughtful and highly personal approach, helping each client understand what suits them and express who they are with clarity and confidence.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="about-chapter" id="why-not-normal">
      <div class="chapter-number">03</div>
      <div class="chapter-heading">
        <p class="eyebrow">Why Not Normal Style?</p>
        <h2 class="about-fit-title"><span>Not made</span><span>to fit in.</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="chapter-copy">
        <p>Not Normal Style grew from a desire to make personal image consulting feel more approachable, relevant and genuinely personal.</p>
        <p>The Instagram space became an early way to share practical knowledge, offer visual inspiration and open conversations about style beyond trends and expectations. What began as a place to communicate those ideas gradually developed into a broader consulting practice.</p>
        <p>The name reflects the belief at the heart of the brand: personal style should not require someone to follow a formula or disappear into somebody else’s idea of what looks right.</p>
        <p>“Not normal” is not about standing apart for the sake of it. It is about recognising what makes each person individual and giving that identity space to be seen. Because being stylish does not mean looking like everyone else. It means looking and feeling like yourself.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="about-chapter chapter-dark" id="philosophy">
      <div class="chapter-number">04</div>
      <div class="chapter-heading">
        <p class="eyebrow">The Not Normal philosophy</p>
        <h2 class="about-philosophy-title"><span>Your style.</span><span>Your rules.</span></h2>
      </div>
      <div class="chapter-copy">
        <p>My role is not to impose an image or replace one identity with another. It is to help each client see themselves more clearly and make choices that feel natural, intentional and authentic.</p>
        <p>Colour, silhouette and wardrobe are part of that process, but the experience goes further. It is about developing a more confident relationship with your image and understanding how to express who you are in daily life.</p>
        <p>There is no single definition of beauty and no universal formula for personal style. The goal is not to change who you are. It is to make who you are more visible.</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="about-closing about-closing-centred">
      <p class="eyebrow">Not Normal Style · Personal Image Consulting</p>

      <p class="about-closing-copy">Discover a personal image that feels considered, confident and entirely your own.</p>

/*
  ==================================================
  EDITORIAL HERO WITH PHOTOGRAPH
  ==================================================

  On larger screens, the hero uses two columns:
  - the text occupies the left column;
  - Camila's photograph occupies the right column.

  gap: 64px;
  align-items: center;
}

/*
  Keeps the text comfortably readable.
*/
.hero-content {
  max-width: 720px;
}

/*
  Creates a tall editorial frame for the photograph.
  "overflow: hidden" hides any part extending beyond it.
*/
.hero-image {
  height: min(72vh, 780px);
  min-height: 520px;
  overflow: hidden;
  background-color: #e4e1d8;
}

/*
  Makes the image fill its frame without stretching.

  "object-fit: cover" may crop part of the photograph
  while preserving its original proportions.
*/
.hero-image img {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  object-fit: cover;
  object-position: center;
}

===== FILE: ./backup-before-performance-rollback-20261109-231232/js/main.js =====
const yearElement = document.querySelector("#current-year");
const menuButton = document.querySelector(".menu-button");
const navigation = document.querySelector("#main-navigation");

if (yearElement) {
  yearElement.textContent = new Date().getFullYear();
}

if (menuButton && navigation) {
  menuButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const isOpen = navigation.classList.toggle("is-open");
    menuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
  });

  navigation.querySelectorAll("a").forEach((link) => {
    link.addEventListener("click", () => {
      navigation.classList.remove("is-open");
      menuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
    });
  });
}


/* Keep only one detailed service open at a time. */
const serviceItems = document.querySelectorAll(".service-item");
serviceItems.forEach((currentItem) => {
  currentItem.addEventListener("toggle", () => {
    if (!currentItem.open) return;
    serviceItems.forEach((otherItem) => {
      if (otherItem !== currentItem) otherItem.open = false;
    });
  });
});


/* Services category submenu. */
const submenuButton = document.querySelector(".submenu-button");
const servicesSubmenu = document.querySelector("#services-submenu");

if (submenuButton && servicesSubmenu) {
  const closeServicesSubmenu = () => {
    servicesSubmenu.classList.remove("is-open");
    submenuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  };

  submenuButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const isOpen = servicesSubmenu.classList.toggle("is-open");
    submenuButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
  });

  servicesSubmenu.querySelectorAll("a").forEach((link) => {
    link.addEventListener("click", closeServicesSubmenu);
  });

  document.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
    if (event.key === "Escape") {
      closeServicesSubmenu();
      submenuButton.focus();
    }
  });
}


/* Consultation form validation. */
const consultationForm = document.querySelector("#consultation-form");
if (consultationForm) {
  const status = document.querySelector("#form-status");

  const setError = (field, message) => {
    const error = document.querySelector(`#${field.id}-error`);
    field.setAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
    if (error) error.textContent = message;
  };

  const clearErrors = () => {
    consultationForm.querySelectorAll("[aria-invalid='true']").forEach((field) => field.removeAttribute("aria-invalid"));
    consultationForm.querySelectorAll(".field-error").forEach((error) => error.textContent = "");
    status.textContent = "";
  };

  consultationForm.addEventListener("submit", (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    clearErrors();
    let firstInvalid = null;

    const name = consultationForm.elements["full-name"];
    const email = consultationForm.elements.email;
    const confirmEmail = consultationForm.elements["confirm-email"];
    const language = consultationForm.elements.language;
    const message = consultationForm.elements.message;
    const privacy = consultationForm.elements["privacy-acknowledgement"];
    const interest = consultationForm.querySelector("input[name='interest']:checked");

    if (!name.value.trim()) { setError(name, "Please enter your name."); firstInvalid ||= name; }
    if (!email.value.trim() || !email.validity.valid) { setError(email, "Please enter a valid email address, for example name@example.com."); firstInvalid ||= email; }
    if (!confirmEmail.value.trim() || confirmEmail.value.trim().toLowerCase() !== email.value.trim().toLowerCase()) { setError(confirmEmail, "The email addresses must match."); firstInvalid ||= confirmEmail; }
    if (!language.value) { setError(language, "Please select your preferred language."); firstInvalid ||= language; }
    if (!interest) { document.querySelector("#interest-error").textContent = "Please choose what brings you here."; firstInvalid ||= consultationForm.querySelector("input[name='interest']"); }
    if (message.value.trim().length < 20) { setError(message, "Please add at least 20 characters so Camila Rades can understand your enquiry."); firstInvalid ||= message; }
    if (!privacy.checked) { setError(privacy, "Please confirm that you have read the Privacy Policy."); firstInvalid ||= privacy; }

    if (firstInvalid) {
      status.textContent = "Please review the highlighted fields.";
      firstInvalid.focus();
      return;
    }

    status.textContent = "The form is complete. Delivery is not connected in this local preview, so no information has been sent.";
  });
}

      <header class="legal-hero">
        <p class="eyebrow">GDPR information</p>
        <h1>Privacy Policy.</h1>
        <p>Last updated: <span class="legal-placeholder">[INSERT DATE]</span></p>
      </header>

      <div class="legal-introduction">
        <p>This Privacy Policy explains how personal data is handled by Not Normal Style in accordance with the General Data Protection Regulation (EU) 2016/679 (GDPR) and applicable Polish data-protection law.</p>
        <p class="legal-draft-note"><strong>Draft completion notice:</strong> fields in square brackets must be completed before the consultation form is connected and published.</p>
      </div>

      <section><h2>1. Data controller</h2><p>The controller of personal data is <span class="legal-placeholder">[FULL LEGAL NAME OR REGISTERED BUSINESS NAME]</span>, trading as Not Normal Style.</p><ul><li>Business or correspondence address: <span class="legal-placeholder">[ADDRESS]</span></li><li>Privacy contact email: <span class="legal-placeholder">[EMAIL]</span></li><li>Registration or tax details, where applicable: <span class="legal-placeholder">[DETAILS]</span></li></ul></section>

      <section><h2>2. Personal data we collect</h2><p>When you submit a consultation enquiry, we may collect your name, email address, country or city, preferred language, consultation interests, preferred consultation format, timing, referral source and information you choose to include in your message. We may also process technical information necessary to operate and secure the website and form.</p><p>Please do not submit health information, identity documents, payment details or other sensitive personal information through the initial consultation form.</p></section>

      <section><h2>3. Purposes of processing</h2><p>We process personal data to review and understand enquiries, respond to prospective clients, recommend a suitable service or next step, arrange introductory conversations, maintain necessary business correspondence, and protect the website and enquiry process from misuse or security threats.</p></section>

      <section><h2>4. Legal bases under the GDPR</h2><p>Depending on the nature and stage of your enquiry, processing may be necessary to take steps at your request before entering into a contract under Article 6(1)(b) GDPR, or necessary for our legitimate interests in responding to enquiries and managing business correspondence under Article 6(1)(f) GDPR.</p><p>Where we request consent for a separate optional purpose, such as marketing communication, processing will be based on Article 6(1)(a) GDPR. Consent may be withdrawn at any time without affecting processing carried out lawfully before withdrawal.</p></section>

      <section><h2>5. Requirement to provide data</h2><p>You are not legally required to submit a consultation enquiry. However, information marked as required is necessary for us to understand and respond to your request. Without this information, we may be unable to respond or recommend a suitable next step.</p></section>

      <section><h2>6. Recipients and service providers</h2><p>Personal data may be accessed by Not Normal Style and by providers that support website hosting, form delivery, email, scheduling, security or other necessary business operations.</p><ul><li>Website hosting: GitHub Pages</li><li>Form processing: <span class="legal-placeholder">[FORM PROVIDER]</span></li><li>Email provider: <span class="legal-placeholder">[EMAIL PROVIDER]</span></li><li>Scheduling provider, if used: <span class="legal-placeholder">[SCHEDULING PROVIDER OR NOT USED]</span></li><li>Analytics provider, if used: <span class="legal-placeholder">[ANALYTICS PROVIDER OR NOT USED]</span></li></ul></section>

      <section><h2>7. Transfers outside the EEA</h2><p>Some providers may process personal data outside Poland or the European Economic Area. Where required, transfers will rely on an applicable adequacy decision, approved contractual safeguards or another lawful transfer mechanism under the GDPR. This section will be finalised after the providers listed above are selected.</p></section>

      <section><h2>8. Retention</h2><p>Consultation enquiries that do not result in a service will be deleted or anonymised after <span class="legal-placeholder">[INSERT RETENTION PERIOD, FOR EXAMPLE 12 MONTHS]</span>, unless a longer period is necessary to establish, exercise or defend legal claims or comply with legal obligations.</p><p>Business, contractual, tax and payment records may be retained for periods required by applicable law.</p></section>

      <section><h2>9. Your rights</h2><p>Subject to the conditions of the GDPR, you may have the right to request access to your personal data, correction of inaccurate data, deletion, restriction of processing, data portability, or to object to certain processing. Where processing is based on consent, you may withdraw that consent at any time.</p><p>To exercise a right, contact <span class="legal-placeholder">[PRIVACY EMAIL]</span>.</p><p>You also have the right to lodge a complaint with a competent supervisory authority. In Poland, the supervisory authority is the President of the Personal Data Protection Office (UODO), ul. Stanisława Moniuszki 1A, 00-014 Warsaw, Poland.</p></section>

      <section><h2>10. Automated decision-making</h2><p>Not Normal Style does not intend to make decisions that produce legal or similarly significant effects based solely on automated processing. Any future service-matching questionnaire will provide guidance only and will not determine eligibility for a service without human involvement.</p></section>

      <section><h2>11. Security</h2><p>Reasonable technical and organisational measures will be used to protect personal data against unauthorised access, loss, misuse, alteration or disclosure. No electronic transmission or storage method is entirely risk-free, so visitors should avoid including unnecessary sensitive information in an initial enquiry.</p></section>

      <section><h2>12. Marketing</h2><p>Submitting a consultation enquiry does not subscribe you to marketing communications. If newsletters or promotional communication are introduced, separate permission will be requested where required and an unsubscribe method will be provided.</p></section>

      <section><h2>13. Cookies and analytics</h2><p>The policy will be updated to reflect the tools actually used by the website. Non-essential analytics, advertising, embedded scheduling, social-media content or other tracking technologies will not be enabled without appropriate disclosure and, where required, a valid consent mechanism.</p></section>

      <section><h2>14. Policy changes</h2><p>This Privacy Policy may be updated when services, providers or legal obligations change. The current version will be identified by the “Last updated” date at the top of this page.</p></section>

      <section><h2>15. Contact</h2><p><span class="legal-placeholder">[FULL LEGAL OR BUSINESS NAME]</span><br><span class="legal-placeholder">[PRIVACY EMAIL]</span><br><span class="legal-placeholder">[BUSINESS OR CORRESPONDENCE ADDRESS]</span></p></section>
